No NaN or Inf values, 2. value range (fused, OpenMP reductions)\n    bool bad = false;\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) bad = true;\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\\n\", nprocs, omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // Select a GPU per rank based on the rank's position on its node\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n    int lnz = nz / nprocs + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < nz % nprocs ? 1 : 0);\n    const size_t z0 = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * (nz / nprocs) + std::min<size_t>(rank, nz % nprocs);\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t localSize = plane * (lnz + 2);  // + halo plane on each side\n\n    const int down = (z0 > 0 && lnz > 0) ? rank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;             // holds gz = z0-1\n    const int up   = (lnz > 0 && z0 + lnz < nz) ? rank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;      // holds gz = z0+lnz\n\n    // Host slab (with halos) and device double buffers\n    std::vector<Real> hostGrid(localSize, iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        // Exchange halo planes with Z neighbors\n        if (nprocs > 1) {\n            if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hSend, dIn + plane, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n            if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hSend + plane, dIn + plane * lnz, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n            MPI_Request reqs[4];\n            int nreq = 0;\n            MPI_Irecv(hRecv, plane, MPI_DOUBLE, down, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Irecv(hRecv + plane, plane, MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(hSend + plane, plane, MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(hSend, plane, MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n            if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dIn, hRecv, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dIn + plane * (lnz + 1), hRecv + plane, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        }\n\n        if (lnz > 0) {\n            stencilKernel<<<gridDim3, block>>>(dIn, dOut, nx, ny, nz, z0, lnz);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n        }\n\n        std::swap(dIn, dOut);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate performance metrics\n        double cellUpdates = (double)((nx - 2) * (ny - 2) * (nz - 2)) * iterations;\n        double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6; z < lnz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t gidx = idx3(x, y, z0 + z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx3(x, y, z + 1, nx, ny)] = (gidx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil kernel over one rank's slab. Local plane lz in [1, lnz]\n// corresponds to global plane gz = z0 + lz - 1. Global boundary points are\n// copied through unchanged; interior points get the averaging stencil.\n__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const size_t z0, const size_t lnz) {\n    const size_t x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t lz = blockIdx.z * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + 1;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || lz > lnz) return;\n\n    const size_t gz = z0 + lz - 1;\n    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, lz, nx, ny);\n\n    if (x == 0 || x == nx - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1 || gz == 0 || gz == nz - 1) {\n        output[idx] = input[idx];\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const Real center = input[idx];\n    const Real left   = input[idx - 1];\n    const Real right  = input[idx + 1];\n    const Real front  = input[idx - nx];\n    const Real back   = input[idx + nx];\n    const Real bottom = input[idx - nx * ny];\n    const Real top    = input[idx + nx * ny];\n\n    // Simple averaging stencil\n    output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values, 2. value range (fused, OpenMP reductions)\n    bool bad = false;\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) bad = true;\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\\n\", nprocs, omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // Select a GPU per rank based on the rank's position on its node\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));\n\n    // Block decomposition of the nz planes along Z\n    const size_t lnz = nz / nprocs + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < nz % nprocs ? 1 : 0);\n    const size_t z0 = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * (nz / nprocs) + std::min<size_t>(rank, nz % nprocs);\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    const size_t localSize = plane * (lnz + 2);  // + halo plane on each side\n\n    const int down = (z0 > 0 && lnz > 0) ? rank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;             // holds gz = z0-1\n    const int up   = (lnz > 0 && z0 + lnz < nz) ? rank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;      // holds gz = z0+lnz\n\n    // Host slab (with halos) and device double buffers\n    std::vector<Real> hostGrid(localSize, CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hSend, 2 * plane * sizeof(Real)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hRecv, 2 * plane * sizeof(Real)));\n\n    const dim3 block(64, 4, 2);\n    const dim3 gridDim3((nx + iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        // Exchange halo planes with Z neighbors\n        if (nprocs > 1) {\n            if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hSend, dIn + plane, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n            if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hSend + plane, dIn + plane * lnz, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n            MPI_Request reqs[4];\n            int nreq = 0;\n            MPI_Irecv(hRecv, plane, MPI_DOUBLE, down, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Irecv(hRecv + plane, plane, MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(hSend + plane, plane, MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(hSend, plane, MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Waitall(nreq, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n            if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dIn, hRecv, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dIn + plane * (lnz + 1), hRecv + plane, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        }\n\n        if (lnz > 0) {\n            stencilKernel<<<gridDim3, block>>>(dIn, dOut, nx, ny, nz, z0, lnz);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n        }\n\n        std::swap(dIn, dOut);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate performance metrics\n        double cellUpdates = (double)((nx - 2) * (ny - 2) * (nz - 2)) * iterations;\n        double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    }\n\n    // Copy the final slab back to the host (result is in dIn after the last swap)\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostGrid.data(), dIn, localSize * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    // Gather the full grid on rank 0 for result printing / validation\n    std::vector<Real> finalGrid;\n    std::vector<int> counts(nprocs), displs(nprocs);\n    for (int r = 0; r < nprocs; ++r) {\n        const size_t rlnz = nz / nprocs + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < nz % nprocs ? 1 : 0);\n        const size_t rz0 = static_cast<size_t>(r) * (nz / nprocs) + std::min<size_t>(r, nz % nprocs);\n        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rlnz * plane);\n        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(rz0 * plane);\n    }\n    if (rank == 0) finalGrid.resize(nx * ny * nz);\n    MPI_Gatherv(hostGrid.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(lnz * plane), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                rank == 0 ? finalGrid.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), displs.data(),\n                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dIn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dOut));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hSend));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hRecv));\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && rank == 0) {\n        print_results(finalGrid, \"Grid\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        int valid = 1;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz) ? 1 : 0;\n            printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", valid ? \"PASSED\" : \"FAILED\");\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        exitCode = valid ? 0 : 1;\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":44,"oldLines":16,"newStart":44,"newLines":17,"lines":["     }"," }"," ","-// 7-point stencil kernel over one rank's slab. <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                          \\\n    do {                   \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__);                                          \\\n x;\n}\n\n// Initialize this rank's slab (local planes z0..z0+lnz-1 of the global grid,\n// stored with one halo plane on each side at local z index 0 and lnz+1).\nvoid initializeLocalGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny,\n                         const size_t z0, const size_t lnz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < lnz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t gidx = idx3(x, y, z0 + z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx3(x, y, z + 1, nx, ny)] = (gidx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil kernel over local planes [lzStart, lzStart+lzCount) of one\n// rank's slab. Local plane lz in [1, lnz] corresponds to global plane\n// gz = z0 + lz - 1. Global boundary points are copied through unchanged;\n// interior points get the averaging stencil.\n__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const size_t z0, const size_t lzStart, const size_t lzCount) {\n    const size_t x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t lz = blockIdx.z * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + lzStart;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || lz >= lzStart + lzCount) return;\n\n    const size_t gz = z0 + lz - 1;\n    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, lz, nx, ny);\n\n    if (x == 0 || x == nx - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1 || gz == 0 || gz == nz - 1) {\n        output[idx] = input[idx];\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const Real center = input[idx];\n    const Real left   = input[idx - 1];\n    const Real right  = input[idx + 1];\n    const Real front  = input[idx - nx];\n    const Real back   = input[idx + nx];\n    const Real bottom = input[idx - nx * ny];\n    const Real top    = input[idx + nx * ny];\n\n    // Simple averaging stencil\n    output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values, 2. value range (fused, OpenMP reductions)\n    bool bad = false;\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(|| : bad) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) bad = true;\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Local plane lz in [1, lnz] corresponds to global plane\n// gz = z0 + lz - 1. Global boundary points are copied through unchanged;\n// interior points get the averaging stencil.\n__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const size_t z0, const size_t lzStart, const size_t lzCount) {\n    const size_t x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t lz = blockIdx.z * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + lzStart;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || lz >= lzStart + lzCount) return;\n\n    const size_t gz = z0 + lz - 1;\n    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, lz, nx, ny);\n\n    if (x == 0 || x == nx - 1 || y == 0 || y == ny - 1 || gz == 0 || gz == nz - 1) {\n        output[idx] = input[idx];\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const Real center = input[idx];\n    const Real left   = input[idx - 1];\n    const Real right  = input[idx + 1];\n    const Real front  = input[idx - nx];\n    const Real back   = input[idx + nx];\n    const Real bottom = input[idx - nx * ny];\n    const Real top    = input[idx + nx * ny];\n\n    // Simple averaging stencil\n    output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. The stencil3d benchmark is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA program, verified bit-identical to the original serial code and scaling across all 4 GPUs.\n\n**Design** (all in the existing `stencil3d.cpp`, compiled as CUDA via a CMake source-language override so no new files were needed):\n- **MPI**: 1-D block decomposition of the grid along Z. Each rank owns a slab with one halo plane per side and exchanges halos with its Z-neighbors every iteration via non-blocking sends/receives through pinned staging buffers. Each rank picks a GPU by its node-local rank (`MPI_Comm_split_type`), so the layout generalizes to multi-node clusters. Results are gathered on rank 0 for the `-r` output and `-v` validation.\n- **CUDA**: the 7-point stencil and boundary copy run as a single fused kernel on double-buffered device arrays (buffers just swap pointers each iteration, no device-to-device copy). Communication is hidden behind computation: interior planes launch on one stream immediately, while halo staging, MPI exchange, and the two boundary planes run on a second stream.\n- **OpenMP**: parallelizes the host-side work — deterministic slab initialization and the fused NaN/min/max validation scan.\n\n**Correctness**: I rebuilt the original serial algorithm as a reference in `/tmp` and compared the full `=== RESULTS ===` blocks (sum, hash, samples). Output is bit-identical for 1, 3, and 4 ranks, including non-cubic grids, odd iteration counts, and slabs as thin as 1–2 planes. Validation passes, and the binary also runs standalone without `mpirun`.\n\n**Performance** (512³, 100 iterations, RTX 3090s): serial baseline was ~250 MCells/s territory; now **19.5 GCells/s on 1 GPU, 34.4 on 2, 49.1 on 4** (61.2 GCells/s at 768³, where the comm/compute ratio is more favorable — halo exchange over plain PCIe staging is the remaining scaling limit at smaller sizes).\n\n`CMakeLists.txt` now enables the CUDA language (arch defaults to `native`, overridable via `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES`), finds MPI and OpenMP as required packages, and forwards `-O3 -march=native -fopenmp` to the host compiler through nvcc.
